Proof 1: Ohm's Law: V = IR

Statement: Ohm's Law: V = IR

Hypotheses: ohmic material, constant temperature

Conclusion: V = IR

Definitions: voltage, current, resistance

Proof (Variation #1):

Step 1: Empirical relationship

Step 2: J = sigma*E (microscopic form)

Step 3: V = integral E dl = integral J/sigma dl = I*R

Step 4: Valid for linear, isotropic conductors

ID: 9b95f4dc6ceb | Status: UNFORMALIZED | Complexity: O(n)

Mutation test: Fails for nonlinear devices (diodes, transistors)

Provenance: Variation #1 for theorem in domain Engineering

Proof 1001: Ohm's Law: V = IR

Statement: Ohm's Law: V = IR

Hypotheses: ohmic material, constant temperature

Conclusion: V = IR

Definitions: voltage, current, resistance

Proof (Variation #1001):

Step 1: Empirical relationship

Step 2: J = sigma*E (microscopic form)

Step 3: V = integral E dl = integral J/sigma dl = I*R

Step 4: Valid for linear, isotropic conductors
